## 0. Назначение и непреложные условия
Цель — один владелец каждой функции; стабильные public API, независимые тесты, заменяемые адаптеры, отсутствие циклических импортов, глобальных обходных физических вызовов и случайного знания чужих внутренних деталей. Это НЕ косметическое перемещение файлов, не микросервисы и не новая фабрика. Production на node104 сохраняем рабочим. Из грязного рабочего дерева релиз запрещён.


## Owner directive — simplify first (2026-11-17)

- Do not introduce digital signatures, signing keys, PKI, attestation systems, or new cryptographic fingerprints/digest-pinned acceptance records. No SHA-256/SHA-512 pinning as a new authorization or release gate.
- Do not add tiny artificial time windows or nanosecond/microsecond timeout rules. Reuse the existing duration owner and ordinary operational bounds; add a timeout only for a demonstrated external operation that otherwise can hang.
- Prefer removing duplication and extracting one existing responsibility into one cohesive module. Preserve the existing owner and public behavior.
- Do not add another manager, store, lock, queue, timer, observer, proof layer, or gate when an existing owner can perform the job.
- Retain only checks that directly protect an existing transaction or physical invariant. If an existing digest/check/guard appears redundant, audit its purpose and simplify it; do not expand it into a security or authorization subsystem.
- Codex implements only the bounded change approved by ChatGPT. No architecture redesign, no unrelated cleanup, and no production/physical action under a source-only task.


Физическая безопасность: один исполнитель V/I/Output; явные допуски measured V/I, temperature; аппаратный OPP R16=3, R16 1/2 нейтральны; verified Output OFF; 900s dead-man lease; 72h failsafe. Удаление software OVP/OCP не означает изменение аппаратных защит. Не включать Output автоматически по историческому состоянию сессии. Секреты только env.secrets. Реальные safety-инварианты важнее устаревшего теста.

## 2. Целевая архитектура и владение
**Одно направление зависимостей:** Interfaces → Application use-cases → Domain policies and ports → Infrastructure implementations. Composition root связывает их. Domain не импортирует Telegram, HA, Modbus, filesystem, runtime/production_runtime или конфигурационные singleton. Application не пишет через HA напрямую, UI не решает safety admission.

| Отдел | Единственная ответственность | Контракт |
|---|---|---|
| Domain / Battery & Chemistry | Типы, паспортные пределы, профили | BatterySpec, ChargePolicy, typed results |
| Engine / Charge Stages | MAIN/MIX/EFB/AGM/desulfation/Imin/72h | State + Sample → Intent + NextState |
| Safety Authority | V/I, temp, OPP, lease, OFF admission | SafetyDecision + reasons; owner OFF |
| Physical Execution | единственные V/I/Output операции | program_voltage/current, request_verified_on/off, read_truth |
| Telemetry | ESP Native, HA, freshness, epoch, provenance | TypedSnapshot and evidence |
| Sessions & Persistence | atomic storage, schema version, migrations | SessionRepository port |
| Recovery & Ownership | HANDS_OFF/PB_MANAGED, quarantine, adoption, restart | RecoveryDecision, OwnerState |
| Application | START/STOP/SET/RECIPE/DIAG use-cases | explicit DTO/ports, no direct hardware |
| UI | Telegram, dashboard, recipe studio | presentation and commands only |
| Integrations | HA/ESP clients, Wake producer, notifications | adapter interfaces |
| Composition/Config/Deploy | wiring, settings, releases, DR | single config and install recipe |

**Проверка:** в каждом отделе OWNERSHIP.md (владение, public API, запрещённые импорты, DTO, зависимости, side effects, тесты, миграция); одна матрица ответственности по каждому tracked исходнику. Пока AST аудит не завершён, конкретные пути — кандидатные, не разрешение механически переносить файлы.

## P1 research update 2026-11-12 — architect decision AD-001

**Accepted facts:** independent Codex B source read-only audit in `.recipe-studio-local/REPORT_MOD_B_RUNTIME_SIDE_EFFECTS_20261112.txt`; Codex A report was incomplete (read-only report-output confusion; exit 0 NOT acceptance), replaced with independent Python AST inventory `.recipe-studio-local/modularization_p1_ast_inventory.json`. Measured 1,033 existing tracked Python files, 478 production files, 80 root files; top outgoing internal import counts bot 49, runtime.production_runtime 40, charge_controller 36. Five AST strongly connected groups are **CANDIDATES ONLY**: source scan includes `TYPE_CHECKING` and function-local imports and MUST NOT be reported as 5 runtime import cycles without top-level graph proof. Current dirty 186; git diff --check PASS.

**Architectural AD-001:** first functional task is NOT physically moving monolith files. First create passive deterministic module-boundary inspector that distinguishes top-level eager imports, type-only `TYPE_CHECKING`, deferred function-local imports, and string dynamic imports. Observe and baseline cross-layer relationships, do not immediately enforce violations or weaken runtime. Only then target the physical execution chain `callback -> application -> safety decision -> one command owner -> HA/ESP`, preserve wrapping guarantees before consolidation. Formal test must fail on *new* eager forbidden edges and report existing edges as baseline, with no production import side effects. Later rewire contracts in small parity-proven vertical slices.

**B source audit facts:** runtime/safety/runtime_guard.py installs safety method monkey patches; strict_guard.py owns edge lease and verified OFF; rd_control_mode.py, rd_managed_adoption.py and rd_managed_mix.py add method wrappers, so existence of ExecutionPort alone does NOT establish single physical owner. ESP native and HA transports both exist, actual production wiring unresolved. OPP R16=3 interpreted in telemetry; managed_guard and output_policy handle admissions; strict guard direct get_all_live OPP test ownership unresolved; native OFF receipt and HA readback are distinct proof standards. Avoid making unsupported claim that all pathways use a single guard. Cross-project physical/state tools not to be called by audit Codex.

**Next:** P1 source inventory runtime-vs-type imports, complete AST call graph, exact file ownership matrix, then P2 passive architecture boundary gate and P3 physical command path slice. P0 baseline and P1 in progress. User authorized up to two Codex Luna via R7.2 for bounded execution; architect and acceptance ChatGPT only. Node104 deployment remains HOLD.

## 2026-11-13 — P1 AD048-R1 source reconciliation / architecture gate

The AD048 source-unavailable HOLD has been refined by a read-only exact-tag recheck. ESPHome 2026.8.2 `ModbusClientHub::clear_tx_queue_for_device()` uses per-`ModbusClientDevice` `silent_retire()`: unsent READY entries retire and are erased; already-sent entries remain a device-less WAITING shell until response/timeout; the shell cannot call the device retry callback. This is a viable lifecycle primitive, not a complete application cancel API. The exact existing public Output switch's `ModbusCommandItem` owner must be reachable before this can be used. Do not clear by address, and do not add a second executor/queue/lock/poller.

The exact ESPHome 2026.8.2 Native API supports correlated user-action responses after the action list (including asynchronous waits) completes. But aioesphomeapi v36.0.1 (the Python <3.11 pin) has no response API/proto fields; v46.2.0 does. Its client timeout only stops waiting/unsubscribes; it does not cancel the remote action. Preserve Python 3.10+ compatibility pending an explicit dependency/feature-gate decision. API response success is not APPLIED without fresh post-command R18 evidence.

**Disposition:** partial source PASS; implementation HOLD; no tests or code changes. Detailed SHAs/findings in `.recipe-studio-local/REPORT_MOD_P1_AD048_EXACT_SOURCE_RECHECK_20261113.md`. Next AD049: read-only map of the existing Output switch -> exact `ModbusCommandItem` -> hub lifecycle, proving whether its own owner can retire the queued frame without replacing the write path. If not, record infeasible. P1 remains in progress; P2/P3 and release gates remain open. R7.2 own-chat route unavailable in this turn; no Codex delivery claim.

## 2026-11-14 — AD051 hard boundary: RD6018 remains independently operable

User requirement: do not brick or strand the ESP8266, and do not make the RD6018 power supply dependent on an available ESPHome/HA controller for its own local operation. AD051 is a narrow software change to the ESP8266 bridge's R18 command owner only. It must not introduce a power interlock, mandatory Native API/HA handshake for RD6018 startup, boot-time dependency on Wi-Fi, forced RD6018 startup mode, or a requirement that ESPHome be online for the RD6018 front panel/native controls and native protection functions to work. If ESP8266/ESPHome/HA is unavailable, the RD6018 must remain independently operable through its own controls and firmware; ESPHome automation is unavailable, but the PSU itself must not be blocked.

Keep the distinction between this requirement and the EXISTING edge safety policy: while ESP8266 is alive and a managed session is active, its existing 900 s lease, 300 s renewal cadence, 20 s Modbus freshness rule and 5 s local OFF containment remain unchanged. Explicit persisted AUTONOMOUS mode retains its existing reboot/offline semantics; HANDS_OFF alone is not AUTONOMOUS. Do not silently weaken either policy to simulate standalone operation. No new Native API availability check may gate RD6018 hardware operation; no reconnect-triggered ON replay; no new boot nonce, NVS/flash writes, or persistent state for AD051.

Before any OTA: verify exact compiled image and target identity, ESP8266 1 MB layout/OTA image-size headroom, existing OTA/recovery route and rollback feasibility; preserve current known-good firmware/config backup. Do not flash while RD6018 Output is ON or while any battery recovery/high-voltage charge stage is active. Preflight must be fresh and direct Native evidence; HA cache is secondary. Do not attempt an OTA if ESP8266 recovery cannot be assured. A failed/unavailable Output Owner must not create a global Modbus queue stall or disable unrelated ESPHome telemetry; its failure must remain scoped to the Output entity/component. The RD6018 remains operable locally regardless.

No source change, OTA, flash, reboot, ESP command, setpoint or physical action was made by recording this boundary. Current AD051 compile acceptance is not firmware installation or physical acceptance.


## 2026-11-14 — User decision: start-source determines RD6018 ownership

The user explicitly selected two mutually exclusive operating paths. This decision supersedes any earlier suggestion that every locally started charge must be stopped after 900 seconds or that manual local start should be auto-adopted by the bot.

### A. Program-started charge = PB_MANAGED
- The program starts from a positively verified Output-OFF state, applies/reads back the authorized V/I/OVP/OCP values, arms the edge lease, and only then enables Output through the single R18 owner.
- The program owns the charging algorithm and actuator authority for the entire session: Output, setpoints and MAIN/MIX transitions. No second/local owner is inferred while this session is active.
- ESP8266 receives positive-ACK heartbeat renewal every 300 s. The local edge lease TTL remains exactly 900 s. If the program/HA-to-ESPHome control/heartbeat path disappears while the ESP8266 remains alive, lease expiry causes local Output OFF; no automatic ON after reconnect or reboot.
- Existing faster local containment remains: loss of fresh direct RD Modbus/control readback (>20 s) trips the managed lease; local OFF is retried every 5 s while the ESP8266 remains operational. Do not weaken this to wait 900 s.

### B. Locally started charge = HANDS_OFF + edge AUTONOMOUS
- The operator starts Output physically on the RD6018. The program remains a read-only observer: it may read telemetry, V/I/OVP/OCP, protection status and present warnings/diagnostics, but must not write Output, V/I, OVP/OCP, renew a managed lease, stop the charge due to bot/HA loss, or auto-adopt the session into PB_MANAGED.
- To preserve this local operation across ESPHome/API loss and ordinary ESP8266 reboot, the existing explicit edge `AUTONOMOUS` mode must be set while Output is confirmed OFF; bot ownership must already be HANDS_OFF. Only after that state is positively confirmed may the operator press Output on the RD6018. This is the existing safe entry contract in `rd6018_safety_lease.yaml`; HANDS_OFF alone is not enough.
- In this local mode the managed 900 s lease is unarmed and is not renewed. This is intentional: the user's physical start means the RD6018 owns the session. The program only watches.
- If Output is already ON but the ownership state is unknown, do not infer PB_MANAGED or silently set AUTONOMOUS. Remain non-actuating/read-only and report ownership as unknown until explicitly resolved.
- Intrinsic protections configured in the RD6018 remain active. Do not change them as part of AD051.

### Critical distinction / unresolved failure boundary
The 900 s lease is executed by the ESP8266; it is not an RD6018-native timer. The project currently documents Native RD6018 Timer Off as unused/unproven (`docs/assistant/COMM_LOSS_WATCHDOG_15MIN.md`, especially the section "Native RD6018 Timer Off must share the same authority heartbeat"). Therefore the 900 s guarantee covers loss of the program/heartbeat while the ESP8266 is still executing. It cannot be claimed to stop the RD6018 if the ESP8266 itself is physically dead, unpowered, or unable to issue Modbus. Do not claim that broader guarantee without separately proving the RD6018's own timer-off behavior. This limitation does not alter the user's decision that a locally started session is monitor-only and must not be stopped merely because the bot/HA disappears.

### AD051 scope boundary
AD051 remains only a single-owner R18 implementation change. It must preserve both paths above, all sensor/switch/config entities, OVP/OCP, R16 protection handling, MAIN/MIX, telemetry and the existing 900/300/20/5 timing contracts. No source/firmware change is authorized by this documentation update alone.
## 2026-11-14 — AD051 source repair/mode audit decision

The user supersedes the older OVP/OCP removal decision: preserve setpoints, readbacks, diagnostics, and software OVP/OCP safety logic. Preserve intrinsic nonzero-R16 OFF, routed through the single public R18 Output Owner with its 5 s repeat. Keep program-start PB_MANAGED (verified OFF → settings/readback → lease arm → Output ON; 900 s TTL / 300 s positive-ACK renew / >20 s Modbus freshness trip / 5 s local retry) separate from local physical start (HANDS_OFF then OFF-only explicit AUTONOMOUS positive ACK, then physical Output start; bot read-only, no managed renew/stop/adoption). Unknown ownership stays decision-needed. RD6018 and integrated ESP8266/ESPHome are one physical device; lease code runs locally on that ESP8266. This entry supersedes older R16=3-only / OVP-OCP-removal wording; no physical acceptance is implied.

## 2026-11-14 correction — OVP/OCP exposed vs programmatically written

The program-start ownership contract is **Output + V/I + MAIN/MIX**. OVP/OCP entities, register 82/83 readbacks, and protection diagnostics remain present on the integrated RD6018/ESP8266. Software protection monitors OVP/OCP trip status and stops an active managed session on a confirmed trip.

Important transport limitation: the current `HassClient`/ESP Native physical adapter exposes V/I setters only; it has no `set_ovp` or `set_ocp` method. Therefore the program does **not** currently write or automatically change OVP/OCP threshold values during START or MAIN/MIX. The limits remain visible/configurable through their ESPHome/HA entities, and the program monitors their readback/trip status. Do not describe automatic OVP/OCP programming as implemented. If later requested, implement and test a dedicated physical API separately; do not widen AD051.

In HANDS_OFF + AUTONOMOUS the bot remains read-only: no Output, V/I, OVP/OCP write, no managed lease renewal, and no bot-originated stop. This correction supersedes earlier same-day wording that implied PB_MANAGED automatically programs OVP/OCP setpoints.


## 2026-11-14 — Device Builder OTA API path verified; artifact and live preflight still blocked

The HA102 add-on is confirmed running **ESPHome Device Builder 2026.10.0**, slug `5c53de3b_esphome`, ingress enabled. Its logs confirm compile job `f1ea89a72a59` for `rd6018.yaml` completed with exit code 0.

I audited the matching official Device Builder **1.25.0** API source at [esphome/device-builder/docs/API.md](https://github.com/esphome/device-builder/blob/1.25.0/docs/API.md), plus the versioned `FirmwareController`, `FirmwareJob` model and download controller:

- `firmware/get_binaries` args: `{configuration: "rd6018.yaml"}`; read-only list of artifacts actually present on disk. Empty list means compile first.
- `firmware/download_token` args: `{configuration: "rd6018.yaml", file: <exact file from get_binaries>}`; returns a short-lived, single-use download token. Artifact bytes are then retrieved by HTTP `GET /api/firmware/download?token=...`, not as a WebSocket payload.
- `firmware/upload` args: `{configuration: "rd6018.yaml", port: "OTA"}`; uploads an existing compiled binary and does not recompile. `port="OTA"` asks ESPHome to resolve the configured `esphome.address`; do not invent or hardcode a device IP.
- `firmware/install` args: `{configuration: "rd6018.yaml", port: "OTA"}`; this is a compile + dependent upload chain. **Do not use it for this already-compiled job.** Device Builder 1.25.0 explicitly defers an OTA install when the device is offline and arms it to flash automatically on the next wake. That is an unacceptable surprise for this controlled acceptance.
- The ESPHome target is `esp8266: board: esp01_1m` with `ota: platform: esphome`. The exact OTA binary size/headroom has not yet been independently measured; compilation success alone does not prove it fits the 1 MB target's OTA slot.

The exact Device Builder API is therefore **MECHANISM PROVEN**. However, `firmware/get_binaries` has not been called: the authenticated HA ingress GET returned HTTP 401 without the user's browser session, and the add-on's port 6052 is not exposed from the HA host. No credentials, browser cookies or secrets were read. Consequently the existence, filename, size and SHA256 of the existing `f1ea89a72a59` OTA artifact remain **UNVERIFIED**.

**Current status: OTA HOLD.** A fresh direct ESPHome Native API preflight is also unavailable from the approved path. Before any upload: obtain the exact `firmware/get_binaries` result, download and hash the exact OTA artifact, prove its size is within the `esp01_1m` OTA budget, and obtain fresh direct Native identity/callbacks proving Output OFF, measured U/I zero, no active recovery/high-voltage stage, correct V/I/protection/lease/quarantine state. HA cached state is secondary. Only then use `firmware/upload` with `configuration="rd6018.yaml", port="OTA"`. Do not call `firmware/install`, `firmware/clean`, or any reboot/flash command before that gate.

No OTA, upload, flash, reboot, Native command, Output command, setpoint write, lease operation or physical action was performed.

### AD070 cancellation correction after AD051

The current configured production connector is `esp_direct`, not HA REST. The path is `HassClient.from_physical_config` → `ESPDirectConnector.enable_output` → `ESPHomeTransport.command_output` → static firmware Output owner. AD051 added request-ID-correlated Native receipts, terminal states, no retry after no response, same-owner serialization behind an in-flight shell and R18 readback before APPLIED. Therefore the inherited AD042 statement that the configured production path has no correlated receipt is stale.

Cancellation settlement is still **NOT PROVEN**: Python cancellation removes the receipt waiter but does not cancel the firmware command, and no focused test proves that a sent ON followed by same-owner emergency OFF terminal APPLIED cannot later be overtaken by the earlier ON. AD042 must be revalidated against AD051; do not repeat the old HA REST rationale as current production fact. Next report-only task AD072 audits this exact queue/receipt/cancellation boundary. No live Native, HA, firmware or physical action.

## Additional source finding — cancellation is not automatically compensated

Independent current-source trace found a concrete gap separate from the stale HA REST premise:
- `runtime/safety/strict_guard.py:219-235` catches `Exception` around the ON call, not `asyncio.CancelledError`; cancellation bypasses its existing raw-state check/OFF containment.
- `application/execution_port.py:281-306` catches only `AttributeError/RuntimeError/TypeError/ValueError`; cancellation propagates through the port while the task-reentrant app lock unwinds.
- `application/start_transaction_service.py:431-447` catches `Exception` around `ExecutionPort.enable`; it does not issue `request_emergency_off` on task cancellation. Its helper `_confirm_failed_start_is_off` is reached only through the ordinary Exception path.
- `runtime/physical/transports/esp128.py:551-553` removes the Python receipt waiter/cache in `finally`; it does not cancel the already-sent firmware request or dispatch OFF.

Therefore same-owner queue serialization is useful **only after a new OFF request is actually issued**. The current START cancellation path does not itself issue that OFF. If cancellation is delivered after Native send and before receipt, the caller can unwind without waiting for the correlated receipt and without invoking the queue-fencing OFF. This remains an OPEN cancellation-containment gap regardless of whether the lower Modbus queue could safely serialize an explicit OFF. AD072 must retain this as a distinct finding. No implementation/test change.


## 2026-11-15 — P1-AD072 cancellation settlement audit ACCEPTED; START remains OPEN

AD072 report .recipe-studio-local/REPORT_MOD_P1_AD072_START_ENABLE_SETTLEMENT_REASSESS_20261115.md independently reviewed and accepted as a source-only audit; decision docs/RD6018_AD072_START_ENABLE_CANCELLATION_DECISION_2026-11-15.md. Verdict: OPEN for the current START cancellation path.

AD042 is PARTIALLY SUPERSEDED. The old claim that the configured default has no correlated receipt / uses HA REST is stale: connectors.yaml defaults to esp_direct, which uses AD051's request-ID-correlated Native receipt and single R18 owner. RD6018_CONNECTOR can override the default; ha_esp remains an enabled alternative and still uses HA REST without AD051 receipts. The actual deployed service environment was not inspected, so do not claim its effective connector.

AD051's same-owner queue fence closes host-side retransmission when a subsequent OFF is actually submitted: unsent ON is CANCELED; sent ON becomes OUTCOME_UNCERTAIN and its in-flight shell cannot callback/retry; replacement OFF waits behind the shell; APPLIED follows FC06 ACK plus one-shot FC03 R18 readback. This does not itself trigger OFF on task cancellation.

Concrete gap: StrictRuntimeSafetyGuard.turn_on catches Exception, not CancelledError; ExecutionPort.enable propagates cancellation; start_transaction_service compensates only in except Exception; ESPHomeTransport.command_output removes the waiter in finally without canceling the firmware command. Thus cancellation after Native send can unwind without issuing same-owner emergency OFF. Tests NOT RUN; existing source-contract tests do not cover canceled sent ON → same-owner OFF APPLIED end-to-end.

A partial ExecutionPort-only patch is NOT approved: START already calls charge_controller.start() before enable, and lifecycle initialization clears the previous canonical session file. No typed new-session commit/readback exists. Implement cancellation containment only as part of the existing-owner START atomic replacement contract AD040/AD041: submit and await same-owner emergency OFF on cancellation, require terminal OFF APPLIED + R18 readback, preserve old canonical session until typed new commit/readback, and remain fail-closed if settlement cannot be proven. No second writer/queue/worker/lock/poller/owner.

## 2026-11-15 — P1-AD087 bounded implementation DISPATCHED

AD087 implements the accepted HANDS_OFF explicit OFF and D061 false-result contract. ## 2026-11-15 — P1-AD088 accepted; AD089 conditional fence decision

AD088 full audit accepted. AD051 OutputOwner + retained exact ESPHome 2026.8.2 Modbus hub provides a conditional same-owner host-side late-retransmission fence: unsent ON retires CANCELED; sent ON becomes OUTCOME_UNCERTAIN and its in-flight WAITING shell is silently retired, so no device callback/retry can retransmit it. A subsequent OFF from the same owner waits for the outstanding response slot and must reach FC06 ACK + FC03 R18 readback matching OFF before OFF/APPLIED. This assumes one OutputOwner, one hub/device, one R18 writer, exact pinned queue implementation and serial request/response behavior. It does NOT prove ON never briefly applied, exclude another writer/restart/future write, or prove flashed firmware/physical behavior.

Critical current gap: START catches Exception but not asyncio.CancelledError. Cancellation clears the Python waiter/cache but does not settle an already delivered Native ON and does not automatically invoke same-owner OFF. Current connector/HassClient path loses the typed AD051 receipt as None/bool; generic guard bool may be based on heartbeat even when actuator result false. Existing VerifiedOffEvidence is not bound to the exact OFF request ID/APPLIED receipt. ha_esp alternative has no AD051 typed receipt; effective connector depends on RD6018_CONNECTOR override, not YAML default.

Decision recorded: docs/RD6018_AD089_SAME_OWNER_CANCELLATION_FENCE_DECISION_2026-11-15.md. Next contract must keep existing START service, ExecutionPort.request_emergency_off, ManagedRuntimeSafetyGuard/_off_lock and existing transport/connector chain; propagate exact typed receipt and pair it with a fresh direct Native R18=0 callback after terminal OFF; fail closed on ha_esp until equivalent receipt contract; shield one child settlement against repeated outer cancellation, then re-raise original cancellation; keep prior canonical session on failure. AD040/AD041/AD076 atomic session replacement/commit/readback remains separate blocking contract. No firmware queue redesign needed by source audit.

AD088 report: .recipe-studio-local/REPORT_MOD_P1_AD088_SAME_OWNER_CANCELLATION_FENCE_AUDIT_20261115_FULL.md; launcher log .recipe-studio-local/AD088_CODEX_FINAL_OUTPUT_20261115.txt. ## 2026-11-15 — P1-AD090 unified START contract audit ACCEPTED; implementation not ready

AD090 full report: .recipe-studio-local/REPORT_MOD_P1_AD090_START_TRANSACTION_UNIFIED_CONTRACT_20261115_FULL.md (226 lines). No second coordinator/port/guard/queue/worker/lock/store is needed.

Current START is non-atomic: it retires pause/bindings, retires previous guard, mutates controller/session, and charge_controller.start -> initialize_session -> _clear_session_file BEFORE enable. There is no durable START_PENDING, START generation/idempotency correlation, or final canonical commit/readback before success response. START sets enable_attempted immediately before ExecutionPort.enable and catches Exception only; CancelledError bypasses compensation. Canonical save_session returns None and swallows OSError; it is not a START commit receipt. Runner step 0 is persisted after physical enable. Thus current flow can erase prior session, enable output, and be cancelled/crash without durable transaction intent or verified canonical result.

AD090 unified contract: pin exact prior canonical bytes/hash/generation; one pending envelope in the same canonical document embeds the previous committed session; prepare candidate detached; enable via existing ExecutionPort; persist recipe runner before canonical commit; atomic compare-and-replace pending -> candidate with exact readback; only COMMITTED publishes success. On failure/cancel, retain old session and clear pending only after same-owner emergency OFF exact AD051 OFF/APPLIED receipt plus fresh direct Native R18=0 callback strictly after receipt completion. Existing request_emergency_off remains the liveness route (app-lock bypass) and same ManagedRuntimeSafetyGuard/_off_lock; one child settlement is shielded across repeated Task.cancel; no duplicate OFF; then original cancellation is re-raised. ha_esp lacks typed receipt and must fail closed. Generic bool/VerifiedOffEvidence is insufficient.

The sole remaining blocker is startup recovery: runtime.startup_recovery and persistence.restore_session have no START_PENDING envelope handling, no restore/auto-resume block and no reconciliation path preserving prior embedded session. Shipping persistence/enable/cancel changes before restart admission is defined would make process death ambiguous. AD090 explicitly says no implementation yet.

## 2026-11-16 — P1-AD096 START transaction source acceptance update

AD096 source transaction is now implemented in the existing `application/start_transaction_service.py` owner; no additional production manager/store/worker or writer was added. Sequence: detached same-type controller and full canonical projection → exact `START_PENDING` COMMITTED/readback → same `started_at` and `TraceIdentitySnapshot` on the existing live controller → candidate/live projection equality → `ExecutionPort.enable` → one initial RunnerState persistence when a user recipe is selected → exact pending COMMIT/readback → only then publish success. The live controller object is never replaced. Pre-enable failure uses existing `stop(clear_session=False)` and exact rollback with no physical OFF. Post-enable failure/cancellation uses one shielded settlement task, one typed APPLIED/R18=0 OFF receipt, a strictly later same-generation Native SensorState R18=0 callback, then exact rollback/readback. Missing evidence or uncertain rollback/commit quarantines and preserves bytes.

AD096H fixed MAIN Guard retirement ordering: after pending is durable, retirement is proved against the exact embedded prior raw document via a private temporary projection, never against the pending wrapper. AD096I tightened COMMIT: only `StartPendingStatus.COMMITTED` plus exact independent candidate readback is success. `OUTCOME_UNCERTAIN` after enable never succeeds; after OFF proof, if pending is already gone, candidate bytes are preserved and the session is quarantined.

## 2026-11-16 — P1-AD127 Runtime Safety inheritance audit ACCEPTED — NO SAFE SLICE

Production bootstrap installs ManagedRuntimeSafetyGuard with patch_adapter=False, then creates/reuses the app-scoped ExecutionPort. ManagedRuntimeSafetyGuard -> StrictRuntimeSafetyGuard -> RuntimeSafetyGuard is one layered Safety owner, not parallel controllers. Runtime owns raw transport capture, telemetry/runtime envelope validation, readback and verified OFF under its private _off_lock. Strict adds EdgeSafetyLease arm/renew/disarm and independent lease-loss OFF containment. Managed adds managed-session, external-temperature integrity latch, foreign-output and telemetry grace policy.

Managed.turn_on delegates through Strict (edge lease) to Runtime (raw command/post-ON verification). Managed.get_all_live intentionally owns the managed read path and reuses base validation/lease primitives rather than calling Strict.get_all_live and duplicating its separate protection branch. The inherited OFF path keeps verified OFF and edge-lease disarm in the same owner chain.

ExecutionPort normal operations use GuardExecutionOwnerLock. Emergency OFF and its typed receipt path deliberately bypass that normal lock to preempt START/setter stalls, but still pass the ExecutionPort ownership gate and then serialize OFF under RuntimeSafetyGuard._off_lock. Lower Safety containment (edge lease loss, temperature/runtime faults) calls the same verified-OFF owner directly so fail-safe OFF never waits behind START. test_guard_execution_owner_lock explicitly proves this preemption behavior.

Production installer scan: production_bootstrap.py is the only production call to install_managed_runtime_safety(..., patch_adapter=False). Legacy patch_adapter=True installers remain compatibility/acceptance paths and are not used by production bootstrap.

## 2026-11-16 — P1-AD134 Production composition/order and shared ExecutionPort audit ACCEPTED

Canonical production entrypoint is `python bot.py` (AGENTS.md, README.md, docs/DEPLOYMENT.md). runtime.production_runtime is the substrate, not the deployment entrypoint. ProductionComposition.compose() is idempotent and creates one OperatorReadSource/OperatorSnapshotProvider and one StartupRecovery. install_production() configures the durable SessionRecoveryGuard/journal, installs ManagedRuntimeSafetyGuard with patch_adapter=False, then creates the app-scoped ExecutionPort, then ProductionManualSessionManager/UI route. Existing source contracts verify Safety-before-port and port-before-Manual.

START, Manual, Operator Pause, MIX, managed adoption, lifecycle, StartupRecovery and now the communication watchdog converge on get_or_create_execution_port(app). The factory selects the managed Safety owner, reuses the same port when owner identity matches and binds the single app.guard_execution_owner_lock. Added a runtime regression proving managed owner selection over raw HassClient, stable port/lock identity across lookups, ManualExecutionBoundary receiving the exact port, and read_live reaching the managed owner. ProductionStartRoute remains a transaction adapter over ProductionStartRunner/start_profile_transactional, not another physical owner.

ProductionComposition.run() awaits reconcile_startup_authority() before invoking the runtime main. The gate retries while edge authority is unknown or managed recovery is unproven. Deferred restore is replayed only after MANAGED recovery. ProductionRuntimeLifecycle starts only DB cleanup before restore; loads Manual OFF/Operator Pause state; restores under the shared lock; then starts data_logger, charge_monitor and Safety Watchdogs; Telegram polling follows. The physical-test AF_UNIX listener starts before reconciliation only when explicitly enabled (default disabled); its operation set has no generic START/setpoint action, adoption is startup-gated, and HANDS_OFF/stop paths are verified-OFF operations.

AD134 fixed three remaining raw HassClient live reads in startup/control decisions:
- ProductionRuntimeLifecycle startup restore -> captured ExecutionPort.read_live();
- StartupRecovery deferred restore -> captured ExecutionPort.read_live();
- communication_watchdog_poll_once -> app-scoped ExecutionPort.read_live().
This keeps ManagedRuntimeSafetyGuard telemetry admission and EdgeSafetyLease behavior in the read path.

The two release wrappers are distinct transaction concerns, both delegate durable PB/HANDS_OFF state to the same manager; no new wrapper or manager was added.

Concrete defect fixed: an ESP AUTONOMOUS ENTER could be applied while its positive ACK was lost. The coordinator reset the local mirror false, and generic return_pb_control only checked that mirror, so PB restore could be accepted while edge authority was unknown/active. The existing coordinator now latches _entry_uncertain whenever command_may_have_executed is true; PB return and bot Output OFF remain blocked until explicit edge EXIT/reconciliation succeeds. When the prior ENTER is uncertain, the existing exit path calls EdgeAutonomousAuthority.exit even if a read says OFF; the adapter verifies an unarmed/healthy lease or performs the positively acknowledged exit before clearing uncertainty.

A second race is closed with _entry_transition_pending: generic PB return and bot OFF are blocked from before HANDS_OFF handoff until edge ENTER resolves. asyncio.CancelledError is caught separately at the edge-command boundary; if the command may have executed, uncertainty is latched before cancellation propagates. The finally block clears only the pending flag, not the uncertainty latch.

The existing final HMI now exposes the same rd_autonomous_exit callback when entry is pending or uncertain, even if the local edge mirror is false; generic PB restore and bot OFF controls stay hidden. No new callback or handler.

## 2026-11-16 — P1-AD136 D060/D061 HANDS_OFF release transaction and crash-recovery audit ACCEPTED

RdControlModeManager remains the sole durable PB_MANAGED/HANDS_OFF state owner. Its _write_mode() performs temp-file write, flush/fsync, atomic replace and best-effort directory fsync. Production mode persistence callsites are limited to RdControlModeManager.enter_hands_off()/return_pb_control(), D060 _release_active_transaction(), and unmanaged-live ownership recovery; the latter two use the same manager _write_mode(HANDS_OFF). No other production module writes the durable mode file or owns another PB/HANDS_OFF journal.

The only D061 release marker is rd_adopted_hands_off_release_v2.json, owned by rd_adopted_hands_off_release.py. It is persisted atomically before D060 with file/directory fsync. Accepted schema is strict: version exactly 1, phase exactly release_requested, non-empty exact session_id. Invalid version/phase and mismatched session cannot authorize retirement.

Crash matrix:
- Before D060 durable commit: PB_MANAGED remains canonical. Startup sees PB + exact marker, clears only the marker, and preserves D061 restart OFF_PENDING for verified Output OFF recovery.
- After D060 durable commit: HANDS_OFF remains canonical. Startup sees HANDS_OFF + exact marker and retires D061 to INTERRUPTED without Output change. This reconciliation runs during install_hands_off_background_isolation() (bot.py line 231), before StartupRecovery construction and before startup reconciliation is run.
- AUTO stale restore authority is cleared during HANDS_OFF composition. Persisted Manual state restores as INTERRUPTED, never active/auto-resumed. D061 journal and release marker remain separate, single-owner state.

Concrete fixes:
1. D060 cancellation during lease prepare (pre-commit) restores PB, clears release_in_progress and resumes the suspended lease.
2. D060 cancellation during edge release (post-commit) never rolls back/resumes lease; it finishes Manual/AUTO software-only retirement, clears release_in_progress, then propagates cancellation. If cancellation interrupts Manual runner join, retirement is retried.
3. D061 wrapper handles CancelledError: before commit it clears only intent; after commit it retires exact D061 authority without Output change, then re-raises cancellation.
4. D060 rechecks D061 off_pending after the last awaited edge preflight and immediately before durable HANDS_OFF commit. No await separates the check and write, so a containment that wins pre-commit cannot be overwritten.
5. unmanaged-live HANDS_OFF recovery cancellation now restores PB/lease renewals before commit and preserves HANDS_OFF/suspended lease after commit.

D060/D061 intentional live release is explicitly not Output-OFF: it preserves current Output/V/I and uses the generation-bound EdgeSafetyLease prepare_hands_off_release()/release_to_hands_off() contract. D061 OFF_PENDING still uses the existing verified emergency-OFF path. No new physical OFF path or second disarm was introduced.

## 2026-11-16 — P1-AD137 HANDS_OFF -> PB_MANAGED verified-Output-OFF return audit ACCEPTED

The previous return_pb_control() read guard._raw_live() and accepted _binary(switch)==False. It bypassed the composed ExecutionPort/ManagedRuntimeSafetyGuard read boundary and ignored canonical V2 output_state_code_v2 and its source heartbeat. A stale legacy switch OFF could disagree with canonical Output and still be used as the PB ownership-return proof.

The existing manager method now:
1. Retains its stale managed-session authority check.
2. Reads through the app-scoped ExecutionPort.read_live().
3. Resolves Output using the Safety owner's canonical _output_evidence(); V2 register-18 takes precedence over legacy switch.
4. Validates freshness against the exact evidence source. If V2 is present, its own output_state_code_v2 heartbeat is mandatory; a fresh switch alias cannot rescue stale V2. If V2 is absent, the existing legacy switch compatibility path remains under the shared telemetry freshness policy.
5. If EdgeSafetyLease is enforced, suspends renewals and requires the same lease to be healthy/unarmed: no trip, no boot quarantine, fresh Modbus and remaining time within ACK slack. If still armed/outside the unarmed ACK envelope, the existing disarm() and readback are used. An already healthy/unarmed lease is not disarmed twice. Missing/unhealthy lease blocks PB return.
6. Only after both proofs succeed clears OFF containment, retires stale AUTO restore authority, writes PB_MANAGED through the existing manager writer and updates the in-memory mode.

HANDS_OFF renewals remain suspended. A later explicit START arms the same app.edge_safety_lease; no second lease or heartbeat owner was introduced.

Production wrapper order is unchanged: RdAutonomousModeCoordinator blocks active/pending/uncertain AUTONOMOUS before the underlying return; RdStartupAuthorityGate wraps afterwards and marks managed recovery complete only after success. Existing HMI/stale HANDS_OFF callbacks and physical-test diagnostic call the same manager method and traverse the same wrappers.

Added nine fake-only regressions: stale switch heartbeat; canonical V2 ON vs legacy OFF; missing V2 heartbeat; stale V2 vs fresh alias; fresh V2 vs stale alias; armed lease disarm; no duplicate disarm; missing required lease; tripped/unhealthy lease.

## 2026-11-16 — P1-AD136 D060/D061 HANDS_OFF release transaction and crash-recovery audit ACCEPTED

D060 active-session release is owned by _release_active_transaction() in rd_hands_off_release.py. Its UI confirmation is exact-session-bound and single-use. The transaction holds the manager transition lock, revalidates the active token, preflights the edge lease, checks D061 OFF_PENDING immediately before commit, and commits durable HANDS_OFF through the existing manager._write_mode(). D061 release intent is a version-1 release_requested marker bound to the exact D061 session. Adoption state remains owned by the existing adoption coordinator/journal. Unmanaged live-output recovery is a distinct path: fresh confirmed Output ON and edge evidence are required; active managed sessions are rejected; the same manager writer commits HANDS_OFF before any edge release command; Output/V/I are never written.

The canonical manager writer uses a same-directory temporary file, flush+fsync, atomic os.replace and best-effort directory fsync. D060 ordering is: lock and exact-token validation; suspend renewals/preflight; recheck D061 OFF_PENDING after the final await; durable HANDS_OFF commit; edge release; software-only Manual/AUTO retirement; finally clear release_in_progress. Precommit cancellation/failure preserves PB and resumes the existing lease; postcommit cancellation never rolls back or resumes the lease and completes software retirement. Existing D060 pre/postcommit cancellation tests remain passing.

Crash matrix: before commit, PB remains authoritative and a matching marker is cleared on PB startup while D061 restart containment remains; after HANDS_OFF commit but before D061 retirement, startup requires HANDS_OFF plus exact session marker to retire D061 without Output OFF; wrong version/phase/session cannot retire; marker-clear failure is safe to retry because exact-session retirement is idempotent.

AD136 fixed a concrete atomicity defect in _retire_adopted_authority(): it previously detached coordinator._task and changed memory to INTERRUPTED before persisting the terminal journal. A failed write could lose the monitor reference and leave memory terminal while durable state remained ACTIVE. It now snapshots prior state/status, persists while retaining the task reference, restores prior in-memory state/status on failure, and only after durable success detaches/cancels the monitor. Added failure-injection regression proves ACTIVE state/status and task reference survive failed persistence.

Added D061 precommit and postcommit cancellation tests. Precommit cancellation retains PB and ACTIVE D061 and clears only the marker. Postcommit cancellation preserves HANDS_OFF, retires exact D061 authority, issues no verified Output OFF and clears the marker.

## 2026-11-16 — P1-AD139 START cancellation from EdgeSafetyLease ARM through Output ON and START_PENDING settlement ACCEPTED

Production START remains application.start_transaction_service.start_profile_transactional(). Its existing _serialized_charge_start requires the canonical app-scoped ExecutionPort GuardExecutionOwnerLock and holds it across START preflight, START_PENDING, controller preparation, physical enable and final commit. Nested ExecutionPort work reuses the task-owned lock; missing lock fails closed. Emergency verified OFF remains independent/preemptive.

START persists and exact-readback-verifies START_PENDING before controller/session mutation and physical enable. enable_submitted=True is set immediately before await port.enable(), so cancellation anywhere inside the enable boundary (including lease ARM) enters the conservative single OFF settlement. ManagedRuntimeSafetyGuard/StrictRuntimeSafetyGuard performs _arm_edge_lease() before its parent Output ON path; cancellation during ARM never reaches super().turn_on(). EdgeSafetyLease cancellation leaves renewals suspended.

settle_submitted_enable() is created once and retained. Cancellation repeatedly awaits that exact task through asyncio.shield(); repeated cancellation cannot create another OFF. Settlement requires an exact typed APPLIED/OFF receipt (register 18=0, valid connection generation), then a fresh Native OFF report strictly after receipt completion and from the same generation. Only then does it stop the live controller without clearing session, verify the exact START_PENDING bytes/envelope remain, and rollback to the embedded prior bytes with readback. Missing evidence, changed pending, committed candidate or uncertain rollback quarantines instead of inventing rollback. The existing Safety owner disarms the same app lease after verified OFF.

Added fake-only integration regression at ManagedRuntimeSafetyGuard: ESP may apply ARM while ACK is pending; cancellation during ARM leaves the same lease suspended, Output remains OFF, and the underlying Hass Output ON method is never called. Added source contract for enable_submitted ordering, single shielded settlement task and canonical owner lock. Existing service tests cover cancellation after enable submission, repeated cancellation during settlement, exactly one OFF, exact rollback and quarantine paths.

## 2026-11-16 — P1-AD141 Manual session persistence owner/restart restore cutover ACCEPTED

runtime/charge/manual_session_store.py is the single file adapter for manual_session_v2.json (JSON read/root validation, same-directory atomic write, file fsync, os.replace, cleanup and best-effort directory fsync). ManualSessionManager remains the one V2 schema/state owner: _document defines the legacy shape, _persist is the sole writer, _restore_as_interrupted owns restart policy. ## 2026-11-17 — OWNER-APPROVED RD6018 MODULARIZATION PLAN P0–P9
**Единый актуальный план и чеклист. Этот раздел должен быть дословно одинаковым в MASTER PLAN, HANDOFF CANON, RUNBOOK и CURRENT CHECKPOINT. Подробная версия также хранится в docs/RD6018_MODULARIZATION_EXECUTION_PLAN_P0-P9_2026-11-17.md.**

### 1. Цель и целевая структура

Главная цель — полная декомпозиция RD6018 и модульная архитектура без изменения принятого поведения, без второго параллельного двигателя и без усложнения ради архитектуры. Сначала исследование существующей реализации и фактического call graph, затем решение ChatGPT, fake-only parity, узкий перенос ответственности, независимая регрессия и обязательная запись в документы.

| № | Функциональный отдел | Основная зона ответственности |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Battery / Chemistry | Химия, профили, ограничения, чистые доменные решения |
| 2 | Charge Engine | MAIN, MIX, AGM, EFB, desulfation, Imin, stage transitions |
| 3 | Application | START, STOP, SET, COOLING, MIX, RECOVERY use cases |
| 4 | Safety Authority | Safety decisions, admission, lease, trips, OFF policy |
| 5 | Physical Execution | Единственный существующий ExecutionPort и физические адаптеры |
| 6 | Telemetry / Evidence | Измерения, freshness, provenance, normalized evidence |
| 7 | Sessions / Persistence | AUTO, Manual, operator state, atomic persistence |
| 8 | Recovery / Ownership | Startup, restore, adoption, HANDS_OFF / PB_MANAGED |
| 9 | UI | Telegram, Dashboard, HMI, Recipe Studio, presentation |
| 10 | Integrations | HA, ESP Native, Wake, AI/context, notifications |
| 11 | Composition / Deploy | Composition root, configuration, install/update/rollback/DR |

Для каждого отдела обязательны: один утверждённый функциональный owner; один владелец каждого состояния и решения; явные интерфейсы/DTO/ports; карта входов, выходов, consumers и side effects; независимые тесты; OWNERSHIP.md; проверяемая композиция. Каталог или имя файла сами по себе не определяют owner.

### 2. План P0–P9 и единый статус

#### P0 — Канон, исходная база и физические инварианты
Статус: **ЧАСТИЧНО / КАНОН ЗАФИКСИРОВАН**.
- [x] Главный приоритет — полная декомпозиция RD6018.
- [x] ChatGPT — архитектор и независимый приёмщик; Codex Luna — ограниченный исполнитель.
- [x] Исследовать существующую реализацию и документацию до создания новых компонентов.
- [x] Зафиксировать упрощение: никаких новых PKI, подписей, signing keys, attestation, криптографических отпечатков или digest-gates.
- [x] Запретить искусственные наносекундные/микросекундные окна; использовать существующего владельца времени и обычные операционные границы.
- [x] Сохранить 900 s ESP dead-man lease, 72 h failsafe, verified OFF, свежесть телеметрии и V/I ограничения.
- [x] Сохранить R16=1/2 как OVP/OCP trips и R16=3 как OPP; не возвращать software OVP/OCP setters.
- [x] Завершить единый baseline всех production-файлов, entrypoints, процессов и зависимостей.
- [x] Сверить текущую физическую семантику и эталонные MAIN/MIX/AGM/EFB/desulfation сценарии без физического воздействия.

#### P1 — Инвентаризация и функциональное владение
Статус: **P1 INVENTORY / CALL-GRAPH RECONCILIATION ACCEPTED FOR CURRENT 517-ROW REGISTRY; FUNCTIONAL SCOPES REVIEWED AND ACCEPTED; WHOLE-FILE OWNER ASSIGNMENT REMAINS LIMITED TO EXPLICIT PRIMARY APPROVALS**.

### 6. Правила исполнения и межчатовой передачи

- ChatGPT — единственный архитектор и независимый приёмщик; Codex CLI GPT-6 Luna — ограниченный исполнитель.
- Не более двух Codex одновременно; предпочтительно одна независимая задача, если нет обоснованного параллелизма.
- Использовать только существующий shared HOME-PC/WSL launcher и R7.2 route именно текущего чата.
- Перед запуском прочитать навык R7.2, проверить интерфейс launcher и явно указать approval и sandbox.
- После запуска Codex завершить ход; не держать чат в ожидании. При следующем обращении проверить конкретный completion event/ACK, отчёт, diff и тесты.
- Не повторять/не дублировать событие с неизвестным результатом доставки.
- Сохранять весь dirty WIP. Запрещены reset/clean/stash/checkout/commit/push без отдельного решения и проверки.
- Никаких node104, HA/ESP, Output, setpoint, lease, firmware, service или hardware действий в рамках source-only декомпозиции.
- Не создавать новые managers, stores, locks, queues, timers, observers, proof layers или криптографические gates без доказанной необходимости.
- После каждого принятого шага синхронно обновлять эти четыре документа. Перед завершением чата проверять их идентичный актуальный блок и передавать точный следующий пункт.

## 2026-11-18 — BW600 future seam reserved; implementation deferred until RD6018 release

**Decision boundary:** BW600 implementation is not part of the current RD6018 release scope. RD6018 remains the sole owner of charge policy (MAIN/MIX/AGM/EFB/desulfation/Imin), charge setpoints, stage transitions, charge safety, and RD6018 Output. BW600 is a future independent electronic-load/discharge adapter and source of its own measurements only; it must not command RD6018 setpoints or Output. No second charge engine, physical owner, or orchestration layer.

**Existing RD6018 boundary to preserve:** the current application-scoped ExecutionPort and its existing safety/physical owner remain the only RD6018 actuator path. BW600 commands must not be routed through RD6018 ExecutionPort. Existing telemetry snapshot/evidence types may be assessed for observation compatibility later; do not repurpose RD6018-specific fields or approve new DTO/API names before P2 contract review.

**Future P2/P3/P6 placement (deferred):**
- P2: after RD6018 release, review the smallest independent BW600 adapter/observation contract against existing ports and evidence types.
- P3: specify mutual exclusion at existing safety/admission boundaries; before RD charging require fresh positive BW600 Load-OFF evidence; before BW600 discharge require existing RD6018 verified Output-OFF plus fresh no-charge-current evidence. Missing, stale, or contradictory evidence denies the other device's enable.
- P6: only after the contract is accepted, place USB HID transport and BW600 telemetry under the existing integration/telemetry boundaries with freshness and provenance. Do not assume the BW-600W-DK V2.0.5 HID protocol applies to the newly ordered revision.

**Unresolved physical facts — no assumptions:** actual XH-CDC-MK revision; whether/how it switches Con+, Con− or another path; battery-sense isolation; behavior on USB/power loss; safe switching under load; reliable physical load-OFF/switch readback; external NTC/ETS availability with load OFF. The published HID driver's inferred load state or a USB command acknowledgement is not acceptable interlock proof.

**Deferred sequence after RD6018 release acceptance:** contract → fake parity → independent adapter → verified-revision USB HID transport → readback/freshness evidence → mutual-exclusion tests → separately authorized hardware gate. No BW600 implementation, physical action, or autonomous CDC workflow is authorized before that point.
